- **16s-abundance-kmer-filtering**: Use when building or validating a k-mer detection library for baseLess 16S sequence detection on MinION raw signal, where selected k-mers must maximize Smith–Waterman sequence and dynamic-time-warping squiggle dissimilarity. - **16s-amplicon-alignment-to-reference**: Use when full-length 16S amplicon reads from a long-read platform (PacBio/Nanopore) must be aligned to a reference 16S gene to detect intragenomic polymorphisms or derive strain-level substitution profiles in a microbiome sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-alignment-to-reference))
- **16s-amplicon-consensus-calling**: Use when raw, noisy MinION 16S rRNA amplicon reads (2D or 1D2) must be clustered de novo into OTUs and accurate full-length consensus sequences called without reference-based mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-consensus-calling))
- **16s-amplicon-error-benchmarking**: Use when aligning real vs simulated PacBio HiFi amplicon reads (e.g. Zymo/Titan-1, ATCC 16S, Phylotag) to their references and you need to test whether error profiles — especially terminal insertion spikes — are reproduced by a simulator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-error-benchmarking))
- **16s-amplicon-otu-clustering**: Use when raw demultiplexed paired-end 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ reads (e.g. V4 2x250) must be clustered into operational taxonomic units at the 97% identity threshold with mothur, with taxonomy assigned against Silva/Greengenes/RDP training sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-otu-clustering))
- **16s-amplicon-pipeline-evaluation**: Use when you need to decide which 16S OTU-retrieval pipeline preserves raw paired-end read information, or when benchmarking merging-free (CD-HIT-OTU-Miseq/CDSnake) against merging-based pipelines (DADA2, deblur) on mock community datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-pipeline-evaluation))
- **16s-amplicon-profile-correction**: Use when a 16S rRNA amplicon OTU table (biom, QIIME, GAAS, Unifrac or generic format) with read counts and unaltered Greengenes taxonomic assignments needs GCN bias correction, or when 16S qPCR total abundances must be corrected by community-average GCN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-profile-correction))
- **16s-amplicon-quality-filtering**: Use when paired-end 16S FASTQ reads show good base quality over an initial stretch (e.g. ~200 bp R1 / 180 bp R2) followed by a sudden quality drop, and reads must be trimmed and length-filtered before OTU clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-quality-filtering))
- **16s-amplicon-sequence-clustering**: Use when Illumina MiSeq 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ data (primer-removed, paired, trimmed to 250 bases) must be de novo clustered into true sequence variants with one-mismatch children assessed against sequencing error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-sequence-clustering))
- **16s-amplicon-taxonomic-classification**: Use when assessing whether a 16S variable region (e.g. V4) or full-length V1-V9 amplicon can achieve confident species-level taxonomy against a reference database such as Greengenes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-taxonomic-classification))
- **16s-amplicon-taxonomic-profiling**: Use when benchmarking or reproducing taxonomic classification of 16S amplicon FASTQ data from synthetic mock communities across multiple profiler/reference-library combinations at genus and species level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-taxonomic-profiling))
- **16s-amplicon-variant-calling**: Use when calling de novo sequence variants from primer-trimmed, paired Illumina 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ data (e.g., mock communities like PRJEB24409), especially when a conservative, fast, scalable caller with explicit P values is preferred over DADA2 defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-amplicon-variant-calling))
- **16s-copy-number-estimation**: Use when inferring gene family content or 16S copy number for unsequenced organisms on a Greengenes/IMG 16S reference tree, or normalizing 16S OTU tables by predicted copy number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-copy-number-estimation))
- **16s-copy-number-normalization**: Use when you have a closed-reference Greengenes 13.8 OTU BIOM table (e.g., EMP 16S V4) and need to correct 16S copy-number bias or compute per-sample average copy number (ACN) to compare host-associated vs. free-living communities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-copy-number-normalization))
- **16s-denoising-execution-dada2-deblur**: Use when demultiplexed, trimmed paired-end 16S reads must be processed through multiple denoising/clustering alternatives (DADA2, Deblur, vsearch open/closed/de novo) and the resulting representative sequence sets compared via pairwise UniFrac distances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-denoising-execution-dada2-deblur))
- **16s-genus-level-taxonomic-profiling**: Use when 16S rRNA amplicon reads (e.g., QIAseq-panel Illumina libraries from sputum) need genus-level ASV profiling and Greengenes-based taxonomy, or when comparing hypervariable-region accuracy against a mock community. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-genus-level-taxonomic-profiling))
- **16s-metabarcoding-community-composition-analysis**: Use when paired-end 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ data (e.g. Illumina MiSeq V3–V4) from environmental water samples must be summarized as OTUs and phylum-level relative abundances to answer 'what is the prokaryotic composition?' ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-metabarcoding-community-composition-analysis))
- **16s-mock-community-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a DADA2-based amplicon workflow recovers expected taxon counts, strain-level micro-diversity, and abundance patterns from mock communities, or when measuring its wall-clock runtime scaling (1 vs 4 cores) on small datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-mock-community-benchmarking))
- **16s-otu-compactness-analysis**: Use when you have OTUs built at a 97% percent sequence similarity threshold and need to quantify how poorly clustered they are using MSD and BLD metrics, either across 16S hypervariable regions or across taxonomic groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-otu-compactness-analysis))
- **16s-otu-dissimilarity-metric-comparison**: Use when 16S rRNA amplicon reads have already been clustered into OTUs (e.g. QIIME, 97% PSD threshold) and you need to re-evaluate OTU compactness or quantify how strongly MSD and BLD dissimilarities correlate, at read level or aggregated per OTU. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-otu-dissimilarity-metric-comparison))
- **16s-otu-pipeline-performance-ranking**: Use when wall-clock running times of 16S OTU retrieval pipelines (CDSnake, DADA2, deblur, CD-HIT-OTU-MiSeq utilities) must be ranked per dataset from Table 2-style runtime measurements to verify which pipeline is fastest or slowest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-otu-pipeline-performance-ranking))
- **16s-otu-rarefaction-depth-normalization**: Use when benchmarking 16S-based metagenome prediction (e.g. PICRUSt KOs) against observed shotgun KO profiles as a function of sequencing depth, to identify the read depth at which prediction equals or exceeds a rarefied metagenome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-otu-rarefaction-depth-normalization))
- **16s-otu-table-processing**: Use when marker-gene (16S) abundance data must be converted into a PICRUSt-compatible closed-reference OTU table as the first step of metagenome prediction, NSTI calculation, or accuracy-vs-NSTI analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-otu-table-processing))
- **16s-phylogenetic-tree-inference**: Use when raw 16S amplicon reads must be converted into a phylogenetic tree to accompany an OTU/feature table and taxonomy table (e.g., to build a phyloseq object like MiSurv's biom.Rdata example data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-phylogenetic-tree-inference))
- **16s-predictive-metagenome-inference**: Use when input is a PICRUSt-compatible OTU table (BIOM format, closed-reference picked against Greengenes+IMG) and the goal is predicting metagenome/gene family abundance from marker-gene data alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-predictive-metagenome-inference))
- **16s-rdna-functional-profiling**: Use when the input is a 16S rDNA amplicon OTU/ASV abundance table (QIIME 2 feature table, BIOM/TSV) with representative sequences, and the analysis goal is predicted KEGG pathway abundances rather than taxonomy alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rdna-functional-profiling))
- **16s-reference-database-reformatting**: Use when preparing an annotated microbial 16S FASTA database (Greengenes or SILVA) as input to the CDSnake/CD-HIT-OTU-Miseq pipeline, whose DATABASE parameter expects the reordered format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-reference-database-reformatting))
- **16s-region-wise-sample-grouping**: Use when a genus-level ASV table from 16S rRNA amplicon sequencing must be stratified by amplified hypervariable region (V1–V2, V3–V4, V5–V7, V7–V9) before comparing alpha diversity, beta diversity, or taxonomic resolving power between regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-region-wise-sample-grouping))
- **16s-rrna-abundance-threshold-filtering**: Use when running HashSeq's inferTrueSequences on Illumina 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ data and a filtering threshold must be set to remove low-abundance parent variants before LOESS-based error modeling and variant calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-abundance-threshold-filtering))
- **16s-rrna-amplicon-count-comparison**: Use when assembling overlapping paired-end Illumina 16S rRNA amplicon reads (e.g. 108-base M. capsulatus pairs) and deciding whether lowering the PANDAseq quality threshold from 0.9 to a looser cutoff (e.g. 0.6) recovers more sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-amplicon-count-comparison))
- **16s-rrna-feature-table-processing**: Use when a raw 16S rRNA ASV/OTU count table (taxa in rows, samples in columns) plus sample metadata must be prepared for DA testing — e.g., before applying run_all_tools.sh or ANCOM — and decisions on prevalence filtering, rarefaction depth, and zero treatment are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-feature-table-processing))
- **16s-rrna-gene-copy-number-inference**: Use when a 16S rRNA amplicon or qPCR analysis needs GCN estimates for bacterial or archaeal taxa lacking a sequenced genome, and a phylogenetic tree with taxonomy (e.g. Greengenes 99%-identity OTUs) is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-gene-copy-number-inference))
- **16s-rrna-insertion-boundary-detection**: Use when you have curated full-length 16S rRNA gene sequences (e.g. ≥800 bp aligned bases) and need to flag which carry ≥10 bp insertions and report max/mean/sd insertion lengths, or when replicating the Brown et al. 2015 (10.1038/nature14486) CPR insertion statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-insertion-boundary-detection))
- **16s-rrna-kmer-uniqueness-analysis**: Use when validating that a salient 16S k-mer subset (baseLess read_detection k-mers) can support species detection against an arbitrary genomic background, by quantifying unique-k-mer identifiability across simulated mixture sizes from 1 up to 10,000 constituents. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-kmer-uniqueness-analysis))
- **16s-rrna-metabarcoding-pipeline-benchmarking**: Use when you have raw 16S rRNA .fastq reads and need to compare a new metabarcoding pipeline's OTU inference, runtime, and community-composition results against established pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-metabarcoding-pipeline-benchmarking))
- **16s-rrna-multirank-classification-head-design**: Use when building or reconstructing a 16S rRNA classifier that must predict all seven Linnean ranks (Domain through Species) in a single forward pass, or when verifying that a checkpoint exposes exactly seven per-rank output distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-multirank-classification-head-design))
- **16s-rrna-otu-clustering-parameter-tuning**: Use when benchmarking a 16S rRNA metabarcoding pipeline on mock-community FASTQ reads and you must choose or justify OTU/ASV clustering parameters (e.g. Swarm d distance, VSEARCH vs ASV inference) using precision/recall/F1 at genus level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-otu-clustering-parameter-tuning))
- **16s-rrna-primer-binding-assessment**: Use when you have curated 16S rRNA gene sequences (e.g. assembled from metagenomes, ≥800 bp) and need to estimate which taxa a specific primer pair would miss in an amplicon survey. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-primer-binding-assessment))
- **16s-rrna-reference-alignment**: Use when you have full-length 16S rRNA consensus sequences built de novo from nanopore amplicon reads (2D or 1D2) and need a quantitative accuracy metric by aligning them to a SILVA reference of the known organism, e.g., to characterize a reads-per-consensus accuracy curve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-reference-alignment))
- **16s-rrna-reference-alignment-construction**: Use when you need a full-length 16S rRNA reference MSA to serve as the substrate for aligning OTU fragment sequences (e.g. HMP V1V3/V3V5) before computing MSD or BLD between representative and member sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-reference-alignment-construction))
- **16s-rrna-similarity-bucket-stratification**: Use when you have per-sequence species predictions from a 16S rRNA classifier (e.g., DeepTaxa on the Greengenes2 2024.09 69,335-sequence test set) and need to show whether accuracy degrades for test sequences distant from the 277,336-sequence training pool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-similarity-bucket-stratification))
- **16s-rrna-structural-alignment**: Use when assembled 16S rRNA genes from metagenomes contain large insertion sequences (≥10 bp, e.g. CPR self-splicing introns up to 2,004 bp) that would corrupt a naive sequence alignment, and a reliable domain-level or phyla-level phylogeny is the goal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-structural-alignment))
- **16s-rrna-subregion-amplicon-filtering**: Use when full-length 16S reference sequences must be converted into in-silico amplicons for commonly used sub-regions (e.g., V4, V1–V3, V3–V5) to assess how much taxonomic resolution short-read sub-region sequencing loses relative to the full gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-subregion-amplicon-filtering))
- **16s-rrna-taxonomic-annotation**: Use when OTU clusters (e.g. HMP V1V3/V3V5 at 97% PSD) must be taxonomically labeled, or when testing whether representative-based annotation mislabels member sequences within an OTU. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-taxonomic-annotation))
- **16s-rrna-taxonomic-classification**: Use when 16S rRNA FASTA sequences (full-length ~1,500 bp or in-silico V3–V4 ~420 bp amplicons) need hierarchical taxonomic assignment from domain to species against the Greengenes2 2024.09 reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-rrna-taxonomic-classification))
- **16s-subregion-similarity-thresholding**: Use when you have per-sub-region in-silico 16S amplicon FASTA files and need to test whether a given identity threshold (97/98/99%) recaptures the full-length sequence diversity before committing to a marker-gene region or OTU cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-subregion-similarity-thresholding))
- **16s-substitution-error-modeling**: Use when calling intragenomic 16S SNPs from PacBio CCS read alignments: you must distinguish real intra-genome substitutions from systematic CCS errors (e.g., deletions at homopolymers) before counting SNP profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-substitution-error-modeling))
- **16s-taxonomy-assignment**: Reference for assigning taxonomy to 16S rRNA representative sequences when comparing multiple reference databases (e.g., Greengenes vs NCBI RefSeq) or when predicted taxonomic abundances are needed for mock-community accuracy assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/16s-taxonomy-assignment))
- **16s-v4-read-trimming**: Use when processing Illumina 16S V4 amplicon reads (515f/806r) with Deblur, where sequences must be trimmed to 90 bp so identical V4 tag sequences are comparable across multiple studies before merging BIOM tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-v4-read-trimming))
- **16s-v4-tag-sequence-extraction**: Use when starting from a Deblur-generated feature/observation table (e.g., the EMP 90-bp V4 tag table) and you need the deduplicated set of exact amplicon sequences as FASTA queries for reference-database recapture or exact-match analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/16s-v4-tag-sequence-extraction))
- **17-35**: **Evidence to gather before acting:** - Load the workbook with `openpyxl` (not just pandas) to access cell styles. - Identify the exact row indices for headers vs. data rows. - Locate the date column index and the criteria cell values; convert dates to `datetime` objects (handle serial numbers if needed). - Determine the column mapping from data range to output range (e.g., data columns A–E map to ([Hydr0pon1c/Skills-on-the-fly](https://github.com/Hydr0pon1c/Skills-on-the-fly/tree/HEAD/prompt_optimizer/runs/example/spreadsheet/task_skills/17-35) · ★3)
- **17-ai-adr-catalogue**: Use when maintaining a catalogue of consequential AI architecture, model, data, evaluation, safety, and provider decisions. Use architecture-decision-records for general ADRs and ai-agent-adr-catalogue for autonomous-agent decisions. ([peterbamuhigire/chwezi-sdlc-documentation](https://github.com/peterbamuhigire/chwezi-sdlc-documentation/tree/HEAD/09-governance-compliance/17-ai-adr-catalogue) · ★8)
- **17-ai-agent-action-catalogue-spec**: Use when defining the schema-bound tools an AI agent may invoke, including side effects, reversibility, approvals, audit fields, quotas, and kill switches; use agent PRD for user outcomes. ([peterbamuhigire/chwezi-sdlc-documentation](https://github.com/peterbamuhigire/chwezi-sdlc-documentation/tree/HEAD/02-requirements-engineering/17-ai-agent-action-catalogue-spec) · ★8)
- **17-ai-incident-evidence-pack-spec**: Use when producing or updating AI incident evidence-pack specification for capture, chain of custody, retention, redaction, reproducibility, and handover. Use incident-response-runbook for the neighbouring concern; this skill owns the named document contract and its acceptance evidence. ([peterbamuhigire/chwezi-sdlc-documentation](https://github.com/peterbamuhigire/chwezi-sdlc-documentation/tree/HEAD/06-deployment-operations/17-ai-incident-evidence-pack-spec) · ★8)
- **17-arithmetic**: Audit phase 17-arithmetic: Phase 17 — Arithmetic & Derived-Quantity Verification. Use for this phase or its assigned independent blocks; write evidence-based findings only. ([manicko/mko_get_mediascope_data](https://github.com/manicko/mko_get_mediascope_data/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/17-arithmetic))
- **17-check-designs-against-principles-and-standards**: Checks screens and components against the experience principles and the content, accessibility and system standards, with contrast by script and a breach list for a person to decide. Use when screens are ready for checking. Not for making or fixing screens. ([kumargswamy1/sdd-pdlc-agents](https://github.com/kumargswamy1/sdd-pdlc-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/17-check-designs-against-principles-and-standards))
- **17-ci-cd-integration**: Use Claude Code in CI/CD pipelines — GitHub Actions and OpenShift Pipelines — for automated code review, testing, and deployment assistance. ([rhpds/claude-code-courseware](https://github.com/rhpds/claude-code-courseware/tree/HEAD/lola/ccc/skills/17-ci-cd-integration))
- **17-common-result**: Status: ACTIVE AppliesTo: v10 ([devwinsoft/devian](https://github.com/devwinsoft/devian/tree/HEAD/skills/devian/20-domain-common/17-common-result))
- **17-community-update**: Generate engaging community updates for Discord, Telegram, Skool, Instagram, or any community platform - 'Building in Public' style ([RoRoGitr/sigma-protocol](https://github.com/RoRoGitr/sigma-protocol/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/17-community-update))
- **17-compliance-fair-housing**: Compliance guardrail, validation only. Use for reviewing outbound listings, ads, emails, sequences, and criteria language for fair-housing compliance, prohibited language, steering, and required disclosures. ([QuietFireAI/listing-assistants](https://github.com/QuietFireAI/listing-assistants/tree/HEAD/17-compliance-fair-housing) · 2 copies)
- **17-cowork-bash-mount**: Cowork mounts the plugin install dir under /sessions/CODENAME/mnt/.remote-plugins/ read-only. Bundled scripts can be launched via relative path; CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR expands to a Windows path that fails directly. CLI baseline lets both patterns work. See research section 2.10. ([kazukinagata/analyzing-claude-plugin](https://github.com/kazukinagata/analyzing-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/verifier/skills/17-cowork-bash-mount))
- **17-crlf-injection**: 测试 CRLF 注入时使用。Use when 用户提到 CRLF、%0d%0a、HTTP 响应拆分、头注入、Set-Cookie 注入时加载。 ([brilliant751/web-security-skills](https://github.com/brilliant751/web-security-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/17-crlf-injection))
- **17-crypto-basics**: XOR, classical ciphers, hashing, RSA intuition, and randomness pitfalls. Source curriculum: Intro to Cybersecurity (cryptography). Belt path: intro security. ([bshea-1/pwn](https://github.com/bshea-1/pwn/tree/HEAD/skills/17-crypto-basics) · ★2)
- **17-deploy**: Étape 17 (Phase 5 · lancement) de SaaS Factory — Déploiement & mise en ligne (rôle Release Eng / CTO). Exécute le « ship » décidé à l'étape 15 : plan-then-apply de la promotion staging→production, cutover DNS, activation du tracking (analytics + erreurs), et vérification de la santé prod (canary + rollback). Porte de publication avant la mise en ligne réelle. Se déclenche pour « déployer », « mettre en ligne », « ship », après le SEO (étape 16). ([BatiVoiceAI/saas-factory](https://github.com/BatiVoiceAI/saas-factory/tree/HEAD/skills/17-deploy))
